| RZ-V24-C094 | PRESERVE | Garfiel's earlier recognition of trial costs did not authorize denying Emilia's choice or erase Subaru's actual pain. V17 he can rescue children carefully and receive Mimi's comfort, yet bruises her wrist, hides escalation intent and breaks their retreat agreement, exposing her to harm. His fear, family love and later sincere responsibility do not excuse those acts; accepting help and finally seeking treatment are real corrections without a certificate of lasting cure. V18 Garf accepts redirected responsibility and reciprocal help from Reinhard rather than proving sole strongest status; his self-totalized failure does not erase actual rescue work. No new received apology/forgiveness from critical Mimi or sustained phantom cure is shown. V19: Garfiel's courage, skill and grounded healing coexist with fear, Mimi guilt and family concern. Wilhelm's confidence and being engulfed by blood do not produce Mimi's forgiveness, a returned apology or lasting psychological cure. V20: Garfiel now reframes attachments as strength, accepts siblings' help and tends awake Mimi, but still bears severe wounds and she reopens hers.
